### Part 2 top-layout independent review — fresh Phase 8, 2026-10-14

- **Method and scope:** One fresh reviewer performed all research without children, as requested. Started with `docs/agents/ui-patterns.md`; read `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, domain vocabulary guidance, selected [#436](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/436), the ten verbatim requests, and the current approved UI plan. Loaded scoped Impeccable context. Applied code-review's Standards and Spec axes separately in this single session. Reviewed the approved plan against current standards, not as an exception.
- **Selected source:** Reviewed `git diff HEAD -- app/world-7/spelunking/content.tsx components/world-7/spelunking/spelunkingPart2.tsx docs/plans/spelunking-part-2-ui-plan.md` and the complete current production component/integration. The dense source baseline remains `c3c844c7`; its only later committed change is handoff recording `aa606b10`. Current production files match the final packet's retained source byte-for-byte: content SHA-256 `411c29d1497b8f305172b622ea160fd3292fa449c7302f68218cde81b70664af`, component `3db40c8c9fd182a0cd3eafff1bc74ae055b6d2ad2ac26e88ec04016a1c2ab568`.

#### Standards

- **S1 — P2, section spacing has no effective owner.** `app/world-7/spelunking/content.tsx:39,56–79` puts the loaded summary, character section, tabs, and selected content inside a Fragment. Grommet's outer `Box gap="small"` inserts spacers between its direct React children, not within that Fragment. The installed implementation confirms this at `node_modules/grommet/components/Box/Box.js:149–180`. The route therefore declares a gap that does not separate its loaded sections. This conflicts with the caller-owned external-spacing convention in `docs/agents/ui-patterns.md:89–91` and the pattern-reuse intent of CONSIST-01. A future spacing change at the outer Box would still leave these boundaries unchanged. Use a real loaded-content `Box gap="small"` or equivalent explicit caller-owned spacing. No fix was made.
- **Other maintainability findings: none.** The summary export has one actual route caller. Journal and Characters no longer call it locally. All production callers use the two-tab API; the Characters tab, repeated formula tips, rejected notes, and retired visible labels are gone. No compatibility alias, copied mechanic table, generic cap framework, presentation store, extra controls, duplicate formatter, or speculative future abstraction was added.
- **Safeguards and ownership:** Required Spelunking and Players are direct accesses at content `:26–28`. Empty initialized Players and pre-hydration rendering remain supported guards, not impossible missing-domain protection. Limit/progress `undefined` at component `:74–80,86` means genuinely unbounded bonuses under domain `:237–253`. The below-full fill and `<100%` rounding serve explicit display requirements. Above-maximum stamina is observed, not hypothetical. Page arrays and player joins retain direct required access. ### Part 2 latest human feedback and Phase 6 authorization — 2026-10-14

The human selected Part 2 (#436) and authorized a fresh executor to research and plan its UI, then show a browser-rendered preview for approval. This phase is planning only. Read-only production research and disposable preview code are permitted. Preserve Part 1, accepted backend, prior approvals, intervening files, saves, and unrelated work. Part 3 remains unselected; WikiBot is outside authority.

Feedback verbatim:

1. The daily page reads / recharge / all full in sections should be above the tabs like a summary section not inside the tabs
2. Recharge / all full in should be part of one "section" in the summary for stamina so it's clear what it's talking about
3. "All full in" was just an example wording, can we try something better?
4. "Saved snapshot · Assumes unchanged recharge and no spending." and "Finite limits are approached, not reached." are not helpful labels, remove them
5. "No finite limit" is a bit verbose, just say "No soft cap"
6. The "finite limit effect" is overly verbose and hard to reason about without a math degree, simplify it and move it to the top of the page (in a tooltip as well maybe) instead of repeating it for every bar
7. "Not earned" label isn't needed, users can see when level is 0 or bonus is 0 pretty obviously. Maybe we can give it slightly lower opacity for some indication at a glance?
8. Can you look at the outposts page in royal armory and try that for the bar styles on the chapters?
9. You misunderstood my intent with the characters. I didn't want a characters tab, I want them at above the tabs (and below the summary section you will be adding).
10. Also not sure how you followed the style from sneaking/lab, this is nothing like them and their usage of `CharacterBox` style approach. Do better

**Execution boundary:** Research current Sneaking/Lab composition and Royal Armory Outposts in code and browser captures. Summary precedes a persistent eleven-character grid; both precede Tunnels/Journal tabs. Keep all six chapters and twenty bonuses. Preserve skill/EXP, current/maximum stamina, page-location details, saved-snapshot refill semantics, and correct zero-level earned effects. Centralize one simple soft-cap explanation. Return plan, comparative captures, preview references, and observed runtime limits for approval; stop before production changes.

### Part 2 reopened human feedback — Phase 3, 2026-10-14

The human selected Part 2 (#436) and authorized targeted backend-plan revision. Existing backend and UI commits are recorded above. Prior acceptance sections below remain historical.

Feedback verbatim:

1. "Read pages from one character inventory stack. Storage pages must be moved first." isn't a very helpful set of instructions, remove it
2. Looking at the competitor page, for lore they show a "cap" and a progress bar for each bonus. Is there some diminishing returns / maximum value for bonuses? if so we should indicate that on our page as well
3. "No pages in character inventories" isn't very helpful, just hide it when it's not visible. And to be honest you can just show how many pages we have in "storage" and on hover show where it's located
4. Can you try and make the lore cards more "dense". Probably smaller text size will go a long way but overall give it another try with a more dense approach
5. For the character pane, can you look at how we do Sneaking/Lab and follow a similar display model?
6. For the characters (5) will change how you do them for sure but just as additional feedback: Stamina could have just been labeled with `Stamina` and show `{saved}/{maximum}` instead of split on 2 columns, recharge per hour isn't helpful if they all have the exact same value (which they do as far as I can see), and estminated refill can maybe be a separate "summary" top level information (next to the new characters display) which shows time for all to be refilled and maybe on hover show individual players? not sure what's best here. Experiment with what works nicely with (5) and how this information should be displayed.
7. Somewhat inline with the comment above about a "summary", we can probably move the "Daily page reads" into a top level card and make it a lot more dense, right now it's pretty spread out for what little information it gives so I think it can easily become a single card on the summary. It also has redundant information like remaining / used / limit where it can simply be `8/8` and that's it.

Backend planning must distinguish asymptotic formula limits from hard level or effect caps. Include artifact scaling and unbounded bonuses. Competitor presentation is not formula evidence. This phase does not choose or implement the revised UI composition.

**Planning result:** The revised [standalone backend delivery plan](spelunking-part-2-backend-plan.md) is approved by the delegated supervisor and [published](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/436#issuecomment-6160408409). Two serial fresh scouts located existing contracts and the original formula helper. The planning owner inspected original formula branches, the captured chapter helper and page-level assignment, and all 20 original extracted tuples. No code changed or check ran.

**Mechanics for approval:** There are two unbounded linear bonuses, sixteen `decay` bonuses, and two `decayMulti` bonuses. Positive decay formulas approach finite asymptotes but do not reach them at finite levels. The inspected level-update assignment adds gained levels without a clamp. The daily allowance caps reads, not chapter levels. `decayMulti` includes a baseline of one, so Chapter Two's two multiplier limits are `5 * A`, not `4 * A`. The current artifact factor `A` multiplies the entire effect and its limit. All 20 extracted flags enable it. Chapter Five and Six multiplier-worded bonuses use plain `decay`; do not add a baseline based on their wording.

**Exact evidence:** Original [`ArbitraryCode5Inputs.1.22.js`](../game-snippets/players/ArbitraryCode5Inputs.1.22.js#L8-L24):8–24 establishes the three formulas. [`Spelunk.1.22.js`](../game-snippets/spelunking/Spelunk.1.22.js#L8):8, `ChapterBonus`, establishes saved-level input and conditional artifact 35 scaling. [`PageRead.1.22.js`](../game-snippets/spelunking/PageRead.1.22.js#L8):8, `SPELUNKY_PAGE`, assigns the old bonus level plus `PageReadingLVgains`, without a level cap. Original tuples are retained at `tests/results/spelunking-part-2-data.json`, `extractions.chapter_metadata.result`, and match generated `SpelunkingChapterRepo.ts`:13–237. These are retained offline v1.22 source/snapshot artifacts, not newly exercised game parity.

**Responsibility decision:** Existing stacks, Player skill data, saved/maximum stamina, shared recharge, individual refill, and daily allowance suffice for requests 1, 3–5, and 7 and most of 6. Keep layout, wording removal, location disclosure, and formatting in the UI. Add `ChapterBonus.getEffectLimit()` and `getProgressToEffectLimit()` for request 2. Add `Spelunking.getHoursUntilAllFull()` for request 6: maximum individual snapshot estimate, not summed durations or an elapsed-time simulation. Keep these calculations with their domain owners, not in components. Use `dailyReadsRemaining / dailyReadLimit` for the compact `8/8` display.

**Unresolved blockers and limits:** No required static-data or owner-API blocker remains. Revised backend-plan approval and separate fresh Phase 4 authorization are recorded. The three new getters are present and freshly verified; the execution record above retains exact evidence. The paired real save still has only zero refill estimates; positive account refill cannot gain game-evidence coverage without suitable real paired data. Artifact-disabled behavior, performed reads/resets, automatic rollover, and connected live-game parity remain unproved. Historical TypeScript failure and shared UI runtime errors remain recorded, not rerun or waived.

**Execution instructions:** Fresh Phase 4 is separately authorized. Use serial fresh workers, existing paired tests, and the verification steps in the approved plan. Do not rerun the known unrelated TS18047 merely to confirm it. Do not copy static tables, edit generated directories, drive WikiBot, change Part 1, or start Phase 6. Later Phase 6 must experiment with dense Lore, Sneaking/Lab character composition, location tooltips, and summary placement using all seven requests. A combined page count must retain honest location meaning; it must not imply chest-only storage or read eligibility. Finite formula limits are asymptotes, not reachable hard caps; display rounding must not imply completion.

### Part 2 UI independent review — Phase 8, 2026-10-14

- **Method and scope:** Used code-review's separate Standards and Spec axes with fresh reviewers `Part2UIReview.Part2StandardsReview` and `Part2UIReview.Part2OutcomeReview`, started serially. The integration reviewer independently inspected code, owner contracts, the corrected rendered preview, actual-route screenshots, scenario source, result/report, and complete runtime errors. Baseline was accepted backend commit `ac3df4752e3cf76202252f5d0face88db2358676`; the intervening Hole commit `f9efbcf5` was excluded. Reviewed the two scoped tracked UI diffs and the entire untracked `components/world-7/spelunking/spelunkingPart2.tsx`, not only `git diff` output.
- **Sources:** Started with `docs/agents/ui-patterns.md`; read `CODING_STANDARDS.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.md`, domain-vocabulary discovery guidance, DataRow guidance/API, ContentTabs, and the named Sneaking card reference. Loaded Impeccable's existing product/visual context for this scoped Operate surface. Reviewed [#436](https://github.com/Sludging/idleon-efficiency/issues/436) and the standalone approved UI plan against standards rather than treating approval as an exception.

#### Standards

1. **P3 — Remove impossible missing-domain checks from the route.** `app/world-7/spelunking/content.tsx:25–27` declares both initialized `Spelunking` and `Player[]` as possibly undefined, then tests their existence. This violates **DATA-01** (`CODING_STANDARDS.md:41–43`). The actual route provider calls `initAccountDataKeys` before creating the store (`lib/providers/appDataStoreProvider.tsx:25–28`). The factory registers Players and Spelunking (`data/domain/idleonData.tsx:127,170,248–260`); their initializers return an array and the domain (`data/domain/player.tsx:982–983`; `data/domain/world-7/spelunking.tsx:467–497`). Static and live loads reuse that initialized Map (`lib/stores/appDataStore.tsx:97,109`; `data/domain/idleonData.tsx:414–488`). No normal route lifecycle removes either required key. Keep the genuine empty-character/no-account check and hydration gate; use required domain types and direct access. The scenario's deliberate `map.delete('spelunking')` (`scenario-source.txt:234–239` in the actual-route packet) proves the invented branch renders, not that normal loading can omit an initialized domain.
2. **P3 — Access initialized character page stacks directly.** `components/world-7/spelunking/spelunkingPart2.tsx:25–26` adds `?? []` for a loaded player's page-stack array. This also violates **DATA-01**. Players uses IDs `0` through `charCount − 1` (`data/domain/player.tsx:982–983`), Storage builds one inventory for each actual character (`data/domain/storage.tsx:57–74`), and `Chapter.preparePageStacks` maps every inventory, including empty ones (`data/domain/world-7/spelunking.tsx:267–272,594`). Empty inventories already produce empty arrays. Remove the fallback instead of silently treating a broken required-object contract as an empty location.

- **Maintainability and plan judgment:** These are required-object contract findings, not observed wrong values. No further concrete YAGNI, DRY, MODEL-01, or calculation-placement defect was found in the selected UI. Two small views and chapter-local rendering solve current outcomes without a new store, processing hook, generic framework, forecast, or Part 3 control. Repeated fixed grid tracks do not justify another abstraction. The approved plan's required-object rule (`spelunking-part-2-ui-plan.md:94`) already supports these findings; its missing-data verification requirement does not waive DATA-01.

#### Mechanics and evidence for judging the plan

**Original source inspected:** The ready endpoint identified itself as `source: "offline"`, selected character `3`, and scene `ySnowD3`. A read-only expression reported `{title: "Jelly_Operator", version: "1.22"}`. Source queries used the following accessors without executing gameplay handlers:

| Evidence | Exact accessor and decisive finding |
|---|---|
| P2-G1 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Spelunk.toString()`: `ChapterBonus` reads `Spelunk[8][round(4 * chapter + bonus)]`. It evaluates the chapter formula, then multiplies by `1 + ArtifactBonus(35,0)/100` when original tuple position 4 equals 1. |
| P2-G2 | `idleon.getAttr("CustomLists").h.SpelunkChapters`: original tuples align with existing generated `func`, `x1`, `x2`, `x3`, and `requiredPages`. Generated `x3` is tuple position 4, the artifact-applicability flag. It is not missing static data. |
| P2-G3 | Same Spelunk helper: `MaxDailyPageReads` is `5 + 3 * RiftSkillBonus,18(4)`. Source Rift helper returns an unlock indicator; website Skill Mastery returns the full three extra reads. |
| P2-G4 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_38.prototype._event_InvItem4custom.toString()`: the `SPELUNKY_PAGE` branch checks shared usage at `OptionsListAccount[410]`, rejects stacks below 100 pages, selects a chapter through the page item's ID, checks the bonus's minimum stack size, increments shared usage once, and changes that bonus's shared level. This does not make storage or combined stacks directly readable. |
| P2-G5 | Spelunk helper: `StaminaMax` reads each character's own Spelunking level. Its full formula is recorded below. `ShopUpgBonus` leaves upgrades 4 and 5 linear, but multiplies upgrade 61 by saved `Spelunk[4][8]`. `OverstimBonus2` and Maxima-Track's generated description identify this as the Trackometer contribution. |
| P2-G6 | Spelunk helper: `StaminaRegenRate` has shared modifier inputs and no character-level lookup. It starts at five stamina per hour. Its card contribution uses native `CardLv("w7a10")`, capped after multiplication by two, not an equipment-boosted card effect. |
| P2-G7 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_713.prototype._customEvent_SpelunkStuff2.toString()`: conditional refill adds hourly rate times elapsed seconds divided by 3600, then caps saved stamina at its maximum. Excess can feed Overstim. The website estimate must not run those handlers, reconstruct team selection, or claim an active/away-time simulation. |
| P2-G8 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_345._customBlock_getbonus2.toString()`: argument `-1` chooses the maximum effect across characters. `(1,236,-1)` is the first effect of talent 236, Spelunking Specialty, not an achievement. Allocated talents use each contributing character's extra talent levels. |
| P2-G9 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_RoyalG.toString()`: `OutpostROGbon(2,0)` returns 1 unless saved selected Outpost category is 2. If selected, it returns `1 + n / (40 + n)`, where `n = max(1, builtOutpostCount - 6)`. `TotalStatz(4,0)` counts built Outposts. |
| P2-G10 | `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_SushiStuff.toString()`: `RoG_BonusQTY(11,0)` is the unlocked Sushi bonus. `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_RiftStuff.toString()` confirms that mastery source rewards are indicators, while the existing website API supplies their full effect quantities. |

**Maximum-stamina grouping:** Let `level` be the character's nonnegative Spelunking level; `companion` be companion 148's active bonus; `fish` be Advice on Being Tired; `chapter(a,b)` be that owned Journal bonus.

```text
floor(
  upgrade61Raw * trackometerLevel
  + world7StaminaMerit
  + 10 * companion
  + (14 + level
     + upgrade4 * floor(level / 10)
     + chapter(2,0) + masteryMaxStamina
     + upgrade5 + chapter(3,0) + highestSpelunkingSpecialty)
    * (1 + fish / 100)
    * (1 + companion / 100)
)
```

The Trackometer, task merit, and additive companion terms sit outside the two multipliers. Source adds chapter `(3,0)` and the talent effect as flat terms despite their `%` descriptions. Changing their operation from description text would change the requested cap.

**Hourly-recharge grouping:**

```text
5
* (1 + meritocracy17 / 100)
* (1 + legendTalent30 / 100)
* max(1, royalOutpostRechargeMultiplier)
* (1 + sushi11 / 100)
* (1 + (upgrade5 + bubbleY11 + chapter(2,1) + chapter(5,2)
        + masteryRecharge + min(2 * nativeCardLevel, 20)) / 100)
```

This produces one shared rate in the inspected version. Characters still have distinct saved amounts and caps. Refill uses the requested `max(0, maximum - savedCurrent) / hourlyRecharge` estimate. No wall-clock catch-up or live countdown is planned.

### Latest nine-feedback simplification contract — 2026-10-14

This contract replaces the previous nine-point, six-point, and revision 4 presentation specifications where they conflict.
The [current card plan](spelunking-part-1-ui-plan.md) describes delivery. Approval, source provenance, and proof remain in this handoff.

| User feedback | Current contract and implementation |
|---|---|
| 1. Remove Grand tips | Grand found/capacity and Requirements met/not met or Collection complete remain plain text. Remove the information icon, Grand details, and all hidden/popover fallbacks. |
| 2. Simplify discovery tips | Found images are plain actual `IconImage` elements in 32px wrapping Boxes. Missing images retain opacity `0.55`; only they have a tooltip. Its heading is Spawn depth; its body is only `commaNotation(discovery.data.x6)`. No object-identity header or extra fields remain. |
| 3. Hide completed Boss depth | Use `!tunnel.loreCompleted`, not availability. Eight completed cards omit Boss depth. Scoutpost remains available/incomplete with `115`; Exiled Throne remains locked/incomplete with `130`. |
| 4. Strengthen Best depth | `TextAndLabel textSize="xlarge"` supplies the primary value with its default small label. Completed records use two equal columns; incomplete records use three. No empty placeholder remains. Raw values are unchanged. |
| 5. Match game Amber progression | `currentAmberDenomination` selects the highest active account tier. `AmberDenomination` owns image and normalized haul formatting. The human-approved backend, UI cutover, tests, TypeScript, independent review, and final Red-tier rendered proof are complete. |
| 6. Remove top help | Remove Display notes, Shared drops, their components/imports, and replacement or relocated help. Keep domain metadata and actual inline tunnel drops unchanged. |
| 7. Consolidate account totals | One row follows the heading. Its first two entries are Account discoveries and Account Grand Discoveries, with unfiltered found/capacity totals and stronger small numbers. |
| 8. Keep controls in order | Show locked follows both totals in that same row. Keep its initial checked state, availability filter, and existing callback. Add no duplicate or separate controls row. |
| 9. Replace collection tabs | Collection status follows Show locked as an inline label and Grommet Select. Options are All/Found/Missing, initially All. Preserve local controlled state/callbacks and unfiltered totals, requirements, and records. |

**Actual component facts:** `spelunkingDisplay.tsx` uses one row Box with `direction="row"`, `wrap`, `align="center"`, and `gap="medium"`.
The dropdown wrapper is fixed at 130px with `flex={false}`; Select uses `size="small"` and `aria-label="Collection status"`.
Account labels remain small/accent-2; numeric children are small with weight 500.
The row reuses established inline-label and compact-Select patterns rather than adding a control abstraction.
Missing-object tooltip width is at most 360px. Its heading is Spawn depth; its body contains only the formatted depth value.
Removed component-local helpers are GrandDiscoveryDetails, DisplayNotes, and SharedDropDetails; CircleInformation and ContentTabs imports are gone.
Whole-card fading, locked access copy, source art, inline drop rows, raw records, callbacks, and domain/store state remain unchanged.

The user's reason was that these values rarely change and hidden game functions are not reliable extraction targets.

It does not extend to other static game values or reopen current balance, income, shop UI, or Parts 2/3.

**Implemented Amber ownership:** Exported `AmberDenomination` and five frozen singleton definitions live in the existing Spelunking domain.
The denomination owns identity, base-unit divisor, optional upgrade gate, cached immutable 50×50 image data, and `formatAmount(baseAmount)`.
`Spelunking.currentAmberDenomination` selects the highest active tier using existing `getUpgradeBonus`, with Orange as the ungated default.
`SpelunkingDisplay` selects the owner once per render and passes it to each TunnelCard.
Each card delegates image, normalized amount, and unit name to the denomination. Its accessible label uses formatted amount plus denomination name.
The obsolete `getBaseAmberImageData()` API and sole UI call are removed, as is the UI `notateNumber` import.
No compatibility alias, new update hook, mutable tier cache, or per-render denomination copies were added.

|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | Orange Amber | `1` | Default |
| 1 | Blue Amber | `1e3` | Upgrade `20`, bonus at least `1` |

### Part 1 maintainability revision

**Result:** All twelve approved feedback dispositions are implemented. Focused comparisons, TypeScript, and two fresh account-runtime passes succeeded. No implementation blocker remains. Stop before another review or commit.

**Authorization and plan conflicts:** The user approved the proposed dispositions and then authorized the work. This revision replaces the original plan's separately registered preparation hook, mutable requirement snapshots, both-side name normalization, and authored backend presentation sentences. The backend plan now describes direct metadata, cohesive aspect parsing, derived state, and representative tests. Approval of the earlier design was not treated as an exemption from maintainability feedback.

| Feedback | Implemented disposition |
|---|---|

### Part 1 source findings and prerequisites

- **Access versus completion:** `Spelunk[0][t] >= 1` means that tunnel's Lore completion. `CavesUnlocked` starts at one and scans completion slots 0–8; the highest completed scanned index grants count `index + 2`. Tunnel selection accepts indices below that count. Current `unlocked` therefore means completion, not access. Migrate Gallery's tunnel 5 caller and Cards' tunnel 2 caller explicitly to completion when correcting access. Cards migration preserves existing behavior; its game caller was not independently traced. [G6, R3, R12]
- **Released catalogue and ordinary progress:** Ten current tunnel arrays contain 7–16 rows each. Each final row is a Lore boss, not an ordinary collectible. Nonempty metadata already excludes future slots. Ordinary capacity is **105**, not 115; boss destruction writes completion rather than an ordinary name. Preserve Tome's ordinary-found-count and record/shop aggregates. [G6, R6, R13]
- **Grand encoding and ownership:** `Spelunk[44][t]` is an ordered found count. Zero-based ordinal `n` is found when count exceeds `n`; player-facing numbers start at one. Tunnel owns this behavior and the final-object/upgrade requirements. Numbered rows are data-only; no Grand class or named-item catalogue is needed. Use one named handwritten capacity of ten and derive account capacity from released tunnel count. Current capacity is 100. First destruction records ordinary ownership instead. Each further Grand Discovery is twice as rare. [G6]
- **Grand source rules, not export blockers:** Use a named handwritten reference to existing **Grand Discoveries** upgrade 43. Eligibility requires tunnel access, final ordinary ownership, remaining capacity, and that upgrade's bonus at least one. Its metadata gives one bonus per level; `ShopUpgBonus` clamps negative levels to zero and does not modify upgrade 43 in its effective pass. Own parsed upgrade state therefore supplies this gate without character or cross-domain preparation. Existing ordered objects supply the final ordinary identity. No Grand capacity, gate-reference, released-count, or named-catalogue export is required. [G6, R6, R9, R10]
- **Matched metadata audit:** All ten tunnel names, intrinsic Lore descriptions, boss depths, and all discovery names/numeric positions 1–7 match v1.22 after underscore/space, surrounding-whitespace, and source filler normalization. `Kingdom_Flags ` contains trailing whitespace. Match normalized names without conflating tunnel-local discovery indices with global repository indices. Existing values supply base POW/HP inputs, depth scaling, and minimum spawn thresholds; they are not missing exports. [G6, R6, R9]
- **Raw depth and haul convention:** Keep `[1][t]` unchanged: the game's record panel renders raw best depth. Run/end displays instead use raw depth plus one. Spawn thresholds are raw; exported boss encounter depths use the displayed convention. Identify the convention beside contextual thresholds rather than silently incrementing records. `[2][t]` is the largest settled haul after applicable exit tax/bonus; preserve large string handling. [G6, R3]
- **Completed bounded context inventory:** Existing fields cover object POW (power required to damage), HP (object health), raw spawn thresholds, depth scaling, destruction/ordinary ownership, Lore boss/completion/intrinsic unlocks, and the contextual Amber factor. Shared action context covers descent holes, rope removal on attack, safe rope exit, toll settlement, and biggest haul as Amber kept after exit. Shared destruction outcomes include Prisma Bubbles, Exalted Stamp Frags, Snail Mail, Blinding Lantern, Sigil Syrup, Spirit Offering, and Strange Gold Piece. Tunnel-specific outcomes are Rockenstone (0), Sheetmetal (2), Popcorn (4), and Pillario (6). Bling bags present generated loot, not separate discoveries. No probability, quantity, equipment, or shop-progression audit is needed for this inventory. [G6, G7]
- **Static prerequisite supplied:** The user added `data/domain/data/SpelunkingMetadataRepo.ts` with `rewardLabels` and `itemDrops`. It supplies the shared drop-code/display-name mapping and four tunnel/item associations. The handwritten Spelunking domain owns contextual records derived from this export. `W7item0`–`W7item3` retain their source item names; Spelunking context uses the exported reward labels. No Grand export or broader equipment/shop audit is needed. [G7, R14]
- **Account integration settled:** `domainList` already creates Spelunking. `updateIdleonData` finishes all parsing before updates. Register `updateSpelunking(accountData)` immediately before `"cards"` in `postProcessingMap`; it needs only own parsed state and initialized metadata. It then precedes Cards, Tome score, and Gallery consumers. No part 1 registration belongs in `postPostProcessingMap`. [R15]
- **Parity limit:** These findings combine current offline source and direct Wiki reading. They do not pair a same-version game output with its originating save and do not establish live parity. Later implementation needs that paired evidence and two fresh normal-account-factory smoke passes. UI rendering verification belongs to a separately approved UI phase. Journal, stamina, and advanced-state questions remain with their unselected parts, not part 1 blockers.

## 1. What the mechanic does

Spelunking combines character gathering with account progression. Characters gather Spelunky Pages and skill EXP in World 7 caves. Players read pages to improve the shared Lore Journal, and delve through tunnels to break objects, collect Amber, find discoveries, and reach Lore bosses. Gathering and delving are distinct parts of the same feature. [P1, G1–G3]

Gathering efficiency is not delve POW. The game has separate helpers for gathering efficiency, speed, EXP, and page yield, and for delve POW, rock requirements, HP, and Amber. Their full calculations depend on many account systems. The approved website scope explains these terms and mechanics; it does not calculate personalized efficiency or expected run results. [G1, G2]

Amber funds shop upgrades. Upgrade gates, maximum levels, costs, and effects have contextual rules. The existing website's linear `level × bonusPerLevel` helper is not proof of every effective bonus or current price. Descriptive upgrade information remains in scope; simple arithmetic is suitable only when the relevant values and meaning are verified. [G1, G2, R3]

Tunnel records, ordinary discoveries, Grand Discoveries, Lore milestones, Journal chapters, tools, elixirs, Overstim, Trackometer, and Manic Mode all belong to the intended information scope. Current source also contains automatic-tool gates, escape/toll mechanics, special tool actions, intrinsic tunnel drops, and Amber denominations. These mechanics need readable explanations alongside their related feature information, not additional calculators. [G2]

Lore/boss descriptions may include the game's intrinsic text about what a milestone unlocks. That explains the tunnel milestone itself. It does **not** create a downstream rewards section, track another domain's progression, or add links into other website domains. [P1, R6]

## 2. Ownership and saved-value meaning

|Information|Owner and source|What the value means|
|---|---|---|
|Lore Journal chapter progress and owned chapter bonuses|**Account-wide.** The website reads `Spelunk` with `perPlayer: false`. Current `ChapterBonus` uses shared `Spelunk[8][round(4 × chapter + bonus)]`; successful page reading updates that same array. [R3, G2, G3]|Characters share Journal progress. A character gathers or reads pages, but does not own a separate Journal. Static chapter descriptions alone do not establish an owned bonus.|
|Daily page-read limit and used allowance|**Account-wide.** `MaxDailyPageReads` returns `5 + 3 × RiftSkillBonus,18(4)`. The page-use caller compares shared `OptionsListAccount[410]` with that limit, then increments the same counter after a successful read. [G2, G3]|Reads used by one character consume the same allowance checked for another character. It is not a separate daily allowance for every character. The reset caller was not established.|
|Page stacks used for reading|The inspected page-use event reads the current character's `InventoryOrder` and `ItemQuantity`. [G3]|Inventory pages and shared Journal progress are different things. The caller checks page quantity before reading; available pages must retain their actual inventory ownership, not become a fictional Journal-wide balance.|
|Character name, Spelunking level, and skill EXP|Character-owned. The player parser reads `Lv0_<playerID>`, `Exp0_<playerID>`, and `ExpReq0_<playerID>` into skill entries. `SkillsIndex.Spelunking = 19`. Names come from parsed `playerNames`. [R7, R8]|Level is the character's gathering-skill level; EXP is progress in that skill. Current `StaminaMax` separately uses that character's `Lv0[19]`, plus account modifiers. [G2]|
|Saved current stamina|One value per character within account save `Spelunk[3]`. The website copies each entry into its `stamina` array. Current cavern action code debits that character's tool stamina cost, clamped at zero. [R3, G4]|Current stamina **as recorded in the save**, not a stamina upgrade level or Spelunking skill level. The parser's local name `staminaLevels` is misleading. The amount alone does not establish maximum stamina or elapsed refill since the save.|
|Computed maximum stamina|Current `StaminaMax(characterIndex, 0)` uses that character's `Lv0[19]` and shared modifiers. [G2]|The character's calculated stamina cap, distinct from saved current stamina. This deterministic stat is in scope; the current domain does not calculate it.|
|Stamina regeneration rate|Current `StaminaRegenRate` returns a modifier-adjusted rate. The refill caller adds `rate × (_GenINFO[101] / 3600)` to each character's stamina, capped at `StaminaMax`. [G2, G5]|Stamina per hour, not gathering speed or EXP rate. Character rows may repeat a shared rate where the verified formula is account-wide; no unsupported character-specific variation is implied.|
|Time until full|Derived from saved current stamina, verified cap, and applicable refill rate. [G2, G5]|For positive applicable rate, `max(0, maximum − current) / rate` hours under unchanged refill conditions and no stamina spending. Zero when already full; an unavailable or nonpositive rate must not produce a fabricated countdown. This is a deterministic refill duration, not a predicted delve result or live-readiness rating.|
|Tunnel records, Lore flags, shop levels, and ordinary discoveries|Shared `Spelunk` state. Website mappings include depths `[1]`, hauls `[2]`, upgrade levels `[5]`, discoveries `[6]`, and Amber `[4][0]`. Current `DoWeHaveLoreN1` checks `[0][t] ≥ 1`; `CavesUnlocked` derives cave count from these flags. [R3, G2]|Account progression. Do not assume the existing `tunnel.unlocked = [0][index] > 0` mapping is a complete description of cave access. Its `bossKilled` field is never populated.|
|Grand Discoveries|Shared `Spelunk[44]`; current helper totals these separately. [G1, G2]|Separate from the ordinary discovery list in `[6]`. Ordinary discovery totals must not be labelled Grand Discoveries.|
|Overstim and Trackometer progression|Current helper reads Overstim level from `Spelunk[4][1]` and Trackometer level from `[4][8]`. Their gates/effects use account information. [G2]|Account progression, not character skill levels. Current helper also establishes a Manic unlock gate; its active-state persistence was not traced.|

### Ordinary character rows, not “contribution”

A useful character list means **character name, Spelunking skill level, saved current stamina, computed maximum stamina, stamina regeneration rate, and time until full**. Skill EXP may accompany the level as saved skill progress. Saved values and computed values must remain distinguishable. These rows explain each character's current progression and stamina; they do not assign contribution scores, participant status, readiness ratings, or recommendations. [R3, R7, R8, G2, G4, G5]

Maximum stamina and refill displays are approved deterministic calculations, even when their actual formulas use account modifiers. Formula/caller mapping must verify all modifiers, elapsed-time units, refill conditions, cap behavior, and any away-time handling before presenting a timer as current. The duration above is anchored to the saved amount unless elapsed refill is verified; this brief does not promise a live countdown or infer time passed since the save. Remaining mapping work belongs to later approved backend research/planning, not an exclusion from character information.

The user established that the game's participant-selection UI is not present in cloudsave. Therefore the website cannot reconstruct that selection from the available save. Runtime UI helpers and temporary actor fields do not change that fact. There is no “select characters” control, simulated team, inferred participant list, or website action to alter the game's selection.

## 3. Intended information and read-only website interactions

The desired outcome is: **“What is my current Spelunking progression, and what does the game's information mean?”** Browsing and filtering recorded/static information are website interactions; spending, equipping, reading pages, selecting a team, or starting a run are game interactions only.

|Feature information|Read-only website interaction|Evidence and current input coverage|
|---|---|---|
|Tunnels and Lore bosses: names, intrinsic descriptions, boss depths, access requirements, best depth, biggest haul, and progression milestones|Browse tunnel details. Use **Show locked**, enabled by default. Inspect the relevant milestone without predicting success.|Static tunnel metadata and saved records exist. Current source resolves access versus completion: the parser's `unlocked` currently means Lore completion. Part 1 must correct access and preserve completion consumers. [R3, R6, G6]|
|Ordinary discoveries and Grand Discoveries: identity, tunnel context, requirements, and found/missing progress|Browse entries; filter found/missing or locked entries. Keep the two discovery types visibly distinct.|Ordinary ownership is parsed, but bosses must be separated from collection capacity. Grand `[44]` is an ordered count per tunnel; encoding is resolved but not implemented. Tunnel-owned behavior uses named handwritten capacity and upgrade reference, not new Grand exports. [R3, G6]|
|Amber and shop: saved currency, denominations, upgrade names, current level/max, unlock requirements, and game effect descriptions|Inspect an upgrade or currency explanation; filter locked or completed upgrades. Show a verified next cost/affordability only where its current inputs support easy arithmetic.|Base Amber, purchased levels, and static upgrade metadata exist. Current helper exposes contextual costs/effects and denomination gates; full current price/currency semantics are not established by the website's linear helper. [R3, G2]|
|Gathering and character information: cave/page information, skill terminology, page uses, character name/level/EXP, saved current stamina, computed maximum stamina, regeneration rate, and time until full|Read gathering explanations and ordinary character rows. Browse page and cave information; inspect verified current stamina stats and refill duration.|Character skill levels/EXP and saved stamina have verified meanings. Maximum stamina, regeneration rate, and refill duration are approved deterministic displays, subject to formula/caller mapping. Personalized gathering efficiency, page-yield/EXP-rate calculators, hypothetical teams, and run simulations remain excluded. [R3, R7, R8, G2, G4, G5]|
|Lore Journal and pages: chapter identity, static benefits, owned progress, page requirements, inventory quantities, and shared daily reads|Browse chapters and their bonuses. Distinguish an owned chapter level from a possible benefit; inspect the account-wide read allowance.|Static chapter descriptions exist. Journal `[8]` and daily counter `OptionsListAccount[410]` are source-backed but not parsed into the current Spelunking domain. Inventory quantities retain their actual character ownership. [R3, G2, G3]|
|Tools and elixirs: identities, effects, unlock requirements, ownership, slot rules, saved equipped state where verified, and automatic-tool rules|Read effect and rule descriptions; browse owned/locked entries. No tool/elixir selection or hypothetical loadout.|Current helper has tool gates, elixir ownership/effect/slot helpers, saved `Spelunk[7]` equipped-state checks, and runtime actor state. The current domain does not parse these feature fields; actor-only effects are not save-backed ownership. [G2, R3]|
|Delve rules: object POW/HP, stamina costs, depth progression, escape/toll behavior, special actions, and intrinsic drops|Read concise game-rule and item/action descriptions within the related feature information.|Current helper establishes these mechanic families. No run simulation, drop forecast, personalized damage calculation, or strategy optimizer. [G2]|
|Manic Mode, Overstim, and Trackometer: purpose, gates, saved levels/progress, and static effects|Browse explanations and account progression; show locked mechanics by default with the same optional filter.|Current v1.22 helper establishes unlocks and Overstim/Trackometer saved inputs. Manic active-state persistence and complete current UI semantics remain factual gaps. [G2]|

Approved calculations include verified record/count totals, shared remaining-read allowance, maximum stamina, stamina regeneration rate, and time until full. It does not authorize cross-domain efficiency calculations or replacing contextual game formulas with guesses. No proposed website control modifies a game save.

The information scope covers the **entire feature**. Missing domain mappings are research/handoff facts, not a proposal to ship an incomplete placeholder page or ask the user to accept a smaller feature.

## 4. Supported inputs, missing mappings, and factual gaps

**Existing static metadata, not missing data:** Generated schemas and repositories already provide tunnel names, boss depths, intrinsic Lore descriptions and discoveries; discovery identities plus numeric fields; chapter names, bonus text, `requiredPages`, `func`, and numeric parameters; shop names, descriptions, base costs, increments, maximum levels and required-upgrade gates; and elixir indices/descriptions. The editable domain initializes tunnels, chapters, and shop upgrades, but does not initialize the existing elixir repository. Part 1 discovery numeric meanings are now mapped; other generic fields still need their own source comparison. An unclear field name is not proof that its value is absent. [R3, R6, R9, R10, G6]

**Already parsed:** legacy tunnel `unlocked` labels representing Lore completion, best-depth/haul records, ordinary discovery ownership, purchased shop levels, base Amber, per-character saved current stamina, and character skill data. Existing aggregates include deepest depth, biggest haul, upgrade-level total, and ordinary discovery total. Access is not correctly represented yet. These are descriptive records, not forecasts. [R3, R7, R8, G6]

**Save parsing gaps:** shared Journal chapter levels in `[8]`, shared daily reads used in `OptionsListAccount[410]`, Grand Discoveries in `[44]`, tools/elixirs and their verified saved state, Overstim/Trackometer, and remaining advanced-mode state are not represented by the current Spelunking domain. Static chapter benefits and elixir descriptions already exist; absent ownership/progress parsing must not be called missing static data. Manic active-state persistence still needs a save-versus-runtime distinction. [G2–G4, R3, R9, R10]

**Calculation/caller mapping gaps:** The domain does not calculate maximum stamina, stamina regeneration rate, or time until full. Current source establishes `StaminaMax`, `StaminaRegenRate`, stamina debits, and capped refill arithmetic. Exact modifier inputs, elapsed-time accumulation, refill eligibility/pauses, and away-time behavior remain to be mapped for part 2. Contextual shop prices/effects remain part 3 work. Part 1 access/completion meanings are resolved above; implementation must correct the domain labels while preserving completion consumers. [G2, G4–G6, R3]

**Static audit boundary:** The completed part 1 inventory covers tunnel/object/Lore inputs and the finite contextual outcomes above. It identifies one remaining shared-drop identity mapping, not missing Grand rule exports or per-discovery drop fields. Equipment, full shop progression, and advanced-mode entries belong to their other delivery parts. A missing mapping or unclear numeric field alone is not proof of absent static data. [G6, G7, R6, R9, R10, R14]

**External bonus dependency, not feature coverage:** Big Fish is a separate World 7 account domain in the website. It already parses `Spelunk[11]` and initializes its own generated advice metadata. The seven advice entries span several systems; only “Advice on Being Tired” and “Advice on Sleeping” explicitly concern Spelunking. `StaminaMax` consumes `BigFishBonuses(1, 0)`, so the relevant stamina modifier belongs in calculation/data mapping. This does not authorize a standalone Big Fish upgrade catalogue, progression table, or downstream link on the Spelunking page. Helper placement alone is not UI ownership evidence; the separate website domain establishes the scope boundary without assuming an in-game NPC name. [G2, R11]

**Remaining factual questions:**

1. Part 1's access, completion, Grand encoding, requirements, and bounded context inventory are resolved. The user supplied the missing contextual metadata export. Capacity ten and upgrade identity 43 remain approved handwritten mechanic rules.
2. Which event resets shared daily page-read counter `OptionsListAccount[410]`, and what is the exact reset behavior?
3. Which persistent values represent current Manic activity and remaining advanced-mode progress, versus temporary runtime UI state?
4. Part 1's finite item/action context is recorded above. Do the other delivery parts' tool, shop, and advanced entries match current content and displayed meanings?
5. What precise refill conditions and elapsed-time semantics apply to each character, including any away-time handling?

These questions concern source semantics, parsing, calculations, and unaudited data coverage. They do not reopen the approved character-stat/timer scope, spoilers, excluded optimizers/simulations, or participant controls.

## Sources and evidence limits

### Current offline game source

- **O1:** [Offline tool README](../../sub-projects/idleon-offline/README.md):3,42–47,56–58 — stateful headless original game assets; documented version query, `POST /exec` expression interface, and cloud-save endpoint. This is not a live connected game.
- **O2:** [Offline server](../../sub-projects/idleon-offline/server.cjs):64–75 — expression request and result handling.
- **O3:** [Extraction helper README](../../tests/helpers/README.md):26–38,77–84 and [helper](../../tests/helpers/game-data-extractor.js) — offline URL and expression transport. Observed version query: `idleon.getGameVersion()` returned `{title: "Jelly_Operator", version: "1.22"}`.
- **G2:** Read-only `POST http://127.0.0.1:3101/exec`, inspected 2026-10-11. `idleon.findFunction("Spelunk")` returned `ActorEvents_579`; actual offline source accessor `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Spelunk.toString()` returned the current helper. Relevant branch names: `CavesUnlocked`, `DoWeHaveLoreN1`, `StaminaMax`, `StaminaRegenRate`, shop/tool/elixir helpers, Amber helpers, advanced-mode helpers, `GrandDiscoveries_Total`, gathering helpers, `ChapterBonus`, `MaxDailyPageReads`, and the Big Fish helper family. This source was inspected, not added as a new permanent capture.
- **G3:** Same offline `/exec`, `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_38.prototype._event_InvItem4custom.toString()`. The `SPELUNKY_PAGE` branch checks/increments `OptionsListAccount[410]`, uses character inventory quantities, and updates shared `Spelunk[8]`. This is direct page-reading caller evidence, not an ownership inference from the allowance formula alone.
- **G4:** Same offline `/exec`, `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_713.prototype._customEvent_SpelunkStuff2.toString()`, corroborated by `_customEvent_InsideCavernStuff`. Tool actions check and debit `Spelunk[3][characterIndex]`; `StaminaMax` instead reads character `Lv0[19]` and account modifiers. The callers establish saved stamina amount semantics, not live website readiness.

- **G5:** Same read-only offline `/exec`, current `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_713.prototype._customEvent_SpelunkStuff2.toString()`. Refill adds `StaminaRegenRate(characterIndex, 0) × (_GenINFO[101] / 3600)` to `Spelunk[3][characterIndex]`, capped at `StaminaMax`; overflow can feed Overstim when its shop gate is unlocked. This supports the hourly rate and cap distinction. Exact elapsed-time accumulation and all refill conditions were not fully traced.
- **G6:** Read-only offline `/exec`, inspected 2026-10-12, version **Jelly_Operator v1.22**. Source accessors: `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_579._customBlock_Spelunk.toString()` and `idleon.allScripts.ActorEvents_713.prototype` methods `_event_4`, `_event_5`, `_event_6`, `_event_7`, and `_customEvent_SpelunkStuff`. Current temporary captures: [audit evidence](local://spelunk-part1-current-evidence.md), [helper](local://spelunk-part1-helper-v1.22.js), [destruction/Grand caller](local://spelunk-part1-713-_event_4.js), [records/collection caller](local://spelunk-part1-713-_event_5.js), [Grand popup](local://spelunk-part1-713-_event_6.js), and [exit settlement](local://spelunk-part1-713-_customEvent_SpelunkStuff.js). `_event_4`:26–34 establishes boss completion, ordinary ownership, gate 43, and capacity ten. `_event_5`:73–91 establishes separate capacities and ordered Grand state; `_event_6`:31 supplies tunnel/number identity. Helper:38–40 establishes the unchanged upgrade 43 effective gate. The earlier proposed typed Grand exports were a policy interpretation, not missing game-source evidence; the current human decision withdraws both. These temporary artifacts are not permanent repository captures or paired live-save/output evidence.
- **G7:** Completed bounded part 1 context audit against the same v1.22 captures. `_event_4`:9–20 builds shared destruction drops and maps item codes; the World 7 item branch limits outcomes to tunnel indices 0, 2, 4, and 6. [Static capture](local://spelunk-part1-static-v1.22.json):77–89 contains `CustomLists.Spelunky[5]` names. Helper:24–29 supplies spawn and POW/HP/Amber input meanings; :62–64 supplies holes, rope, and toll rules. Exit settlement:109,125–135,147 supplies raw best-depth records, retained loot, successful-exit Amber bonus, largest settled haul, and displayed run/end depth. This closes the finite context inventory without calculating personalized rates or auditing equipment/shop progression.

## Part 3 UI acceptance and local commit — 2026-10-16

**Acceptance:** The human accepted the Part 3 UI result and its stated check limits. The fresh independent Phase 8 review found zero Standards and zero Spec findings. This acceptance covers only the selected Part 3 UI layer; accepted Part 1/2 work and the accepted Part 3 backend remain unchanged.

**Accepted outcome:** The production route shows all 68 shop upgrades, all 12 elixirs, seven tools, Amber and verified artwork, saved equipment and advanced state, filters, and per-view scroll retention. Corrected Trackometer effects show `+3% / +24%` and `+2% / +16%`; unowned values remain zero. Owned generic bonuses stay paired with intrinsic wording. Existing Tunnels and Journal views remain available.

**Accepted evidence:** The exact-source packet [2026-10-16T00-22-09-182Z-1925](../../.ui-verification/runs/2026-10-16T00-22-09-182Z-1925/) retains production screenshots and source provenance. Scenario assertions passed for all 68 Shop rows, 12 Elixirs, seven tools, advanced values, filters, scroll restoration, and retained views. No check was rerun for acceptance or commit.

**Accepted limits:** Unknown source quantities display `?`; generic owned bonuses remain paired with intrinsic text; unsupported next cost and affordability are omitted. Evidence uses one retained, unpaired save and offline Jelly_Operator v1.22, not connected-game parity. Shared Nav hydration, NitroPay, CORS, `ERR_FAILED`, HTTP 403, and the wider fixture-pairing failures remain unwaived. No TypeScript, build, or lint claim is made.

**Preservation and stop:** No issue was updated or closed; no push, PR, or deployment occurred. Commit only the accepted Part 3 UI layer locally. Preserve unrelated work and the user's index. Stop after the local commit.
